## `AGO-1080-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Homogeneity Tester
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Feed Mixing Homogeneity & Mycotoxin ELISA Testing
- **Intro:** Homogeneity Tester — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Feed Mixing Homogeneity & Mycotoxin ELISA Testing — Pre-Start Safety Verification and LOTO: LOTO lock must be rated for 480 VAC; dust layer thickness ≤ 0.8 mm on surfaces.
- **Key tolerance:** LOTO lock must be rated for 480 VAC; dust layer thickness ≤ 0.8 mm on surfaces.
- **Standards:** `ISO 6498:2012`, `ISO 14675:2003`, `ISO 4833-1:2013`, `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`

## `AGO-1080-MGT-R4-MGT-SC-02`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `MGT`
- **Role:** Dust-Explosion Safety Officer
- **Scenario:** 4M1E Engineering Change (ECN) Containment Approval & 8D/CAPA Root Cause Rectification SOP for Feed Mill Combustible Dust Explosion & Explosion Vent Safety Incidents
